\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\sqrt{L\wedge T}},

where the second inequality follows from Assumptions
1
.2 and
1
.3, and Lemma
B.4
.

For
\mathbf{J}_{12}
, write

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{J}_{12}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{LNT}\sum_{i,j}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\top}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\right\|_{2}

(B.20)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(1)\frac{1}{LNT}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\left\|\mathbf{F}^{\circ}\diag\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}^{{}^{\circ}\top}\}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(1)\frac{1}{LNT}\|\mathbf{F}^{\circ}\|_{2}\left\{\sum_{j=1}^{N}\|\diag\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}^{{}^{\circ}\top}\}\|_{2}^{2}\right\}^{1/2}\left\{\sum_{j=1}^{N}\|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}\|_{2}^{2}\right\}^{1/2}\|\mathbf{F}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{LT}\sqrt{L\vee T}\cdot\sqrt{L}\cdot\sqrt{T}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{L\wedge T}}\right),

where the third inequality follows from Assumptions
1
.2 and
1
.3 and
\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\|\diag\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}^{\circ\top}\}\|_{2}^{2}=O_{P}(1)
as shown in the proof of Lemma
B.4
.

By noting
i
and
j
being symmetric and following the development of
\mathbf{J}_{11}
and
\mathbf{J}_{12}
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{J}_{13}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N\wedge T}}\right),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{J}_{14}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N\wedge T}}\right).

(B.21)

For
\mathbf{J}_{15}
, write

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{J}_{15}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{LNT}\sum_{i,j}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(1)\frac{1}{LNT}\|\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot\centerdot}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{\centerdot\centerdot\centerdot}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{LN\wedge T}}\right),

where the last step follows from Assumptions
1
.1,
1
.2 and
1
.3.

Similar to the development of
\mathbf{J}_{15}
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{J}_{16}\|_{2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{LN\wedge T}}\right).

For
\mathbf{J}_{17}
, write

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{J}_{17}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{LNT}\sum_{i,j}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{{}^{\circ}\top}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{{}^{\circ}\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\right\|_{2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{L\wedge T}\right),

where the second equality follows from Lemma
B.4
.

For
\mathbf{J}_{18}
, write

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{J}_{18}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{LNT}\sum_{i,j}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(1)\frac{1}{LNT}\|\mathbf{F}^{\circ}\cdot\boldsymbol{\Gamma}^{\circ\bullet}\cdot\mathbf{F}^{\bullet\top}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\sqrt{(L\wedge T)(N\wedge T)}},

where
\boldsymbol{\Gamma}^{\circ\bullet}=\{\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\}_{\|\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\circ}\|_{1}\times\|\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\bullet}\|_{1}}
, and the second equality follows from Assumption
1
.

Similar to the development of
\mathbf{J}_{18}
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{J}_{19}\|_{2}=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\sqrt{(L\wedge T)(N\wedge T)}}.

For
\mathbf{J}_{20}
, write

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{J}_{20}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{LNT}\sum_{i,j}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\right\|_{2}=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\sqrt{(L\wedge T)(LN\wedge T)}},

where the last equality follows from (
B.6
).

Similar to the study of
\mathbf{J}_{20}
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{J}_{21}\|_{2}=O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\sqrt{(L\wedge T)(LN\wedge T)}}.

Similar to
\mathbf{J}_{20}
and
\mathbf{J}_{21}
and by noting
i
and
j
being symmetric, we immediately obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{J}_{23}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\sqrt{(N\wedge T)(LN\wedge T)}},

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{J}_{24}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\sqrt{(N\wedge T)(LN\wedge T)}}.

Following the development of
\mathbf{J}_{17}
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{J}_{22}\|_{2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{N\wedge T}\right).

For
\mathbf{J}_{25}
, write

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{J}_{25}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{LNT}\sum_{i,j}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\right\|_{2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{LN\wedge T}\right),

where the last equality follows from (
B.3
).

Thus, we can conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{C}}-\mathbf{F}\mathbf{H}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{LN}}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{\centerdot\centerdot}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{\centerdot\centerdot}\|+\frac{1}{\sqrt{L\wedge N\wedge T}}\right),

(B.22)

where
\mathbf{H}=\frac{1}{LNT}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot\centerdot}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot\centerdot}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\widehat{\mathbf{V}}^{-1}
. Here, it is noteworthy that
\widehat{\mathbf{V}}
converges to the eigenvalues of
\frac{1}{NT}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot\centerdot}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot\centerdot}
as we have
\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\mathbf{F}=\mathbf{I}_{\ell}+o_{P}(1)
in Assumption
1
.3. In connection with the fact that

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{\mathbf{F}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}+\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1})

\displaystyle\cdot[(\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}+\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1})^{\top}(\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}+\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1})]^{-1}(\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}+\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1})^{\top},

the second result of Lemma
1
follows immediately.
\blacksquare

Proof of Lemma
2
:

(1). Note that we can have the following expansion:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}^{\dagger}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot})^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}^{\dagger}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}

\displaystyle+(\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}^{\dagger}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot})^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}^{\dagger}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ}

\displaystyle+(\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}^{\dagger}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot})^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}^{\dagger}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle+(\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}^{\dagger}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot})^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}.

Our goal is to prove
\max_{i,j}\|\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}\|=o_{P}(1).
Suppose that there exits some
(i,j)
such that
\|\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}\|\neq o_{P}(1)
.

For the term
(\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}^{\dagger}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot})^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}^{\dagger}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}
, we write

\displaystyle\max_{i,j}\|(\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}^{\dagger}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot})^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}^{\dagger}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{i,j}\|(\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}^{\dagger}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot})^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\circ}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}\|

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j}\|(\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}^{\dagger}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot})^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}\|+o_{P}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{P}(1),

where the first inequality follows from
\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\|=o_{P}(1)
by the second result of Lemma
1
, and the second result follows from the fourth condition of (
2.11
) and (
B.22
).

Next, suppose that
\mathbf{x}_{ijt}
is a scalar for simplicity for now only. Then write

\displaystyle\max_{i,j}\frac{1}{T}|\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{i,j}\frac{1}{T}|\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}|+\max_{i,j}\frac{1}{T}|\tr((\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}+\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\circ}}+\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}})\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{i,j}\frac{1}{T}|\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}|+O(1)\max_{i,j}\frac{1}{T}\|\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{i,j}\frac{1}{T}|\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}|+O(1)\max_{i,j}\frac{1}{T}\|\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{P}(1),

where the last equality follows from Lemma
B.2
and Assumption
2
straightaway. Thus,

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}^{\dagger}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot})^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}\|=o_{P}(1).

Therefore, if
\|\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}\|\neq o_{P}(1)
, we conclude that

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}^{\dagger}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot})^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}^{\dagger}(\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ}+\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet})\|\neq o_{P}(1).

Thus, it infers

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}^{\dagger}(\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ},\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet})\|\neq o_{P}(1).

We now show it violates the first result of Lemma
1
and (
2.13
). In view of Lemma
1
and Assumption
1
.4, we can always replace
\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\circ}
and
\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}
with
\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}
and
\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}
to achieve

\displaystyle Q(\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{\centerdot\centerdot},\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{\centerdot\centerdot|\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ},\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}}^{*})\leq Q(\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{\centerdot\centerdot},\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{\centerdot\centerdot}),

where
\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{\centerdot\centerdot|\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ},\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}}^{*}
is obtained by replacing
\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\circ}
and
\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}
with
\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}
and
\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}
. Thus, the result follows.

(2)-(3). Note that by construction,
i
and
j
are symmetric, so we only need to prove one. Without loss of generality, we consider
Step 1
.d.

For
\forall j\in[N]
, write

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{j}^{\bullet}=\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{j}^{\bullet}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}

(B.23)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\top}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\top}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\top}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\top}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\top}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle\coloneqq

\displaystyle\mathbf{W}_{j,1}+\cdots+\mathbf{W}_{j,25},

where the definitions of
\mathbf{W}_{j,1}
to
\mathbf{W}_{j,25}
should be obvious.

For
\mathbf{W}_{j,1}
, write

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,1}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}\right\|_{2}

(B.24)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\max_{j}\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\|\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}\|^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|^{2}),

where the first inequality follows from the triangle inequality and the fact that
\frac{1}{T}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}=\mathbf{I}_{\ell_{j}^{\bullet}}
, and the second inequality follows from Assumption
1
.4.

For
\mathbf{W}_{j,2}
, write

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,2}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\top}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\max_{j}\left\|\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\top}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\left\{\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\|\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}\|^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|^{2}\right\}^{1/2}\left\{\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}\|^{2}\right\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\|\right),

where the second inequality follows from Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, and third inequality follows from the development for
\mathbf{W}_{j,1}
and Assumptions
1
.1 and
1
.2.

Similar to the development of
\mathbf{W}_{j,2}
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,3}\|_{2}=O_{P}\left(\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\|\right).

For
\mathbf{W}_{j,4}
, write

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,4}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(1)\max_{j}\frac{1}{LT}\|\mathbf{X}_{\centerdot j\centerdot}\diag\{\boldsymbol{\beta}_{\centerdot j}^{\top}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{\centerdot j}^{\top}\}\diag\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}^{\circ\top}\}^{\top}\mathbf{F}^{\circ\top}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{LT}\sqrt{LT}\cdot(\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|)\cdot\sqrt{\log(LN)}\cdot\sqrt{L\vee T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\log(LN)}}{\sqrt{L\wedge T}}\cdot\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\right),

where the second inequality follows from Assumptions
1
.3,
1
.4 and
2
.2. Similarly, we obtain that

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,5}\|_{2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\log(LN)}}{\sqrt{L\wedge T}}\cdot\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\right).

(B.25)

Similar to the development of
\mathbf{W}_{j,2}
and noting that
\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\|=O_{P}(1)
by Lemma
1
and Assumption

.3, we obtain that

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,6}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|),

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,7}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|).

For
\mathbf{W}_{j,8}
, using the development similar to those for
\mathbf{W}_{j,4}
, we can write

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,8}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}\right\|_{2}

(B.26)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(1)\max_{j}\frac{1}{LT}\|\mathbf{X}_{\centerdot j\centerdot}\diag\{\boldsymbol{\beta}_{\centerdot j}^{\top}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{\centerdot j}^{\top}\}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{\centerdot j\centerdot}^{\top}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{LT}\sqrt{LT}\cdot(\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|)\cdot\max_{j}\|\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{\centerdot j\centerdot}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\cdot\max_{j}\|\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{\centerdot j\centerdot}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{LT}}\right),

where the second inequality follows from Assumptions
1
.1 and
1
.4. Similarly, we can obtain

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,9}\|_{2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\cdot\max_{j}\|\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{\centerdot j\centerdot}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{LT}}\right).

For
\mathbf{W}_{j,10}
, write

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,10}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\top}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(1)\max_{j}\frac{1}{LT}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{T}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{LN}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{\centerdot\centerdot}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{\centerdot\centerdot}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{L\wedge N\wedge T}\right),

where the second equality follows from Assumption
1
.2, and the last step follows from (
B.22
).

For
\mathbf{W}_{j,11}
, write

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,11}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(1)\max_{j}\frac{1}{LT}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}^{\top}\diag\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}^{\circ\top}\}^{\top}\mathbf{F}^{\circ\top}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\sqrt{LT}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\|_{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{L}}\|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}\|_{2}\cdot\|\diag\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}^{\circ\top}\}\|_{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{F}^{\circ}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{\sqrt{\log(LN)}}{\sqrt{L\wedge T}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{LN}}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{\centerdot\centerdot}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{\centerdot\centerdot}\|+\frac{1}{\sqrt{L\wedge N\wedge T}}\right),

where we have used (
B.22
) and Assumptions
1
.2,
1
.3, and
2
.2.

Similar to the development of
\mathbf{W}_{j,11}
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,12}\|_{2}=O_{P}(1)\frac{\sqrt{\log(LN)}}{\sqrt{L\wedge T}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{LN}}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{\centerdot\centerdot}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{\centerdot\centerdot}\|+\frac{1}{\sqrt{L\wedge N\wedge T}}\right).

Similar to the development of
\mathbf{W}_{j,10}
and by noting
\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\|_{2}=O_{P}(1)
using Assumption
1
.3 and (
B.22
), we can also obtain that

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,13}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\|_{2}\right)=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{LN}}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{\centerdot\centerdot}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{\centerdot\centerdot}\|+\frac{1}{\sqrt{L\wedge N\wedge T}}\right),

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,14}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\|_{2}\right)=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{LN}}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{\centerdot\centerdot}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{\centerdot\centerdot}\|+\frac{1}{\sqrt{L\wedge N\wedge T}}\right).

For
\mathbf{W}_{j,15}
, we write

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,15}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(1)\max_{j}\frac{1}{LT}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{\centerdot j\centerdot}^{\top}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{\max_{j}\|\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{\centerdot j\centerdot}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{LT}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{LN}}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{\centerdot\centerdot}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{\centerdot\centerdot}\|+\frac{1}{\sqrt{L\wedge N\wedge T}}\right),

where the second equality follows Assumption
1
.2 and (
B.22
).

Similar to the development of
\mathbf{W}_{j,15}
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,16}\|_{2}=O_{P}(1)\frac{\max_{j}\|\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{\centerdot j\centerdot}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{LT}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{LN}}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{\centerdot\centerdot}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{\centerdot\centerdot}\|+\frac{1}{\sqrt{L\wedge N\wedge T}}\right).

Similar to the development of
\mathbf{J}_{16}
and invoking Assumption
2
.2, we get

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,17}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{\log(LN)}{L\wedge T}\right),

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,18}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\log(LN)}}{\sqrt{L\wedge T}}\right),

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,19}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\log(LN)}}{\sqrt{L\wedge T}}\right).

By the development similar to
\mathbf{J}_{20}
and invoking Assumption
2
.2, we obtain that

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,20}\|_{2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\log(LN)}}{\sqrt{L\wedge T}}\cdot\frac{\max_{j}\|\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{\centerdot j\centerdot}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{LT}}\right),

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,21}\|_{2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\log(LN)}}{\sqrt{L\wedge T}}\cdot\frac{\max_{j}\|\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{\centerdot j\centerdot}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{LT}}\right).

Similar to the development of
\mathbf{W}_{j,15}
and by noting
\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\|_{2}=O_{P}(1)
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,23}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{\max_{j}\|\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{\centerdot j\centerdot}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{LT}},

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,24}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{\max_{j}\|\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{\centerdot j\centerdot}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{LT}}.

For
\mathbf{W}_{j,25}
, we write

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{W}_{j,25}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}\right\|_{2}

(B.27)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(1)\max_{j}\frac{1}{LT}\|\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{\centerdot j\centerdot}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{\centerdot j\centerdot}^{\top}\|_{2}=O_{P}\left(\left[\frac{\max_{j}\|\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{\centerdot j\centerdot}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{LT}}\right]^{2}\right),

where we have used Assumption
1
.1.

Finally, we focus on
\mathbf{W}_{j,22}
.

\displaystyle\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}.

Note that

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{LT}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{LT}\cdot\frac{1}{T}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}-\mathbf{F}\mathbf{H})\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\max_{j}\frac{1}{LT}\cdot\frac{1}{T}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}\mathbf{H}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{C}-\mathbf{F}\mathbf{H}\|_{2}+O_{P}(1)\max_{j}\frac{1}{T}\|\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}\|_{2},

where the second inequality follows from Assumptions
1
.2 and
1
.3. Similarly, it is easy to know that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{LT}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{T}\|\mathbf{C}-\mathbf{F}\mathbf{H}\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{T^{2}}[\max_{j}\|\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}\|_{2}]^{2}\right).

Therefore, we conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}\mathbf{V}_{j}^{\bullet}-\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|+\frac{\sqrt{\log(LN)}}{\sqrt{L\wedge T}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}+\frac{\max_{j}\|\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{\centerdot j\centerdot}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{LT}}+\frac{\max_{j}\|\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}\|_{2}}{T}\right).

The results then follow immediately.
\blacksquare

Proof of Theorem
1
:

(1) We next investigate the asymptotic distribution.

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}=(\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot})^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{Y}_{ij\centerdot},

where
\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}=(\widehat{\mathbf{C}},\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\circ},\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet})
. Note that

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}+(\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot})^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}

(B.28)

\displaystyle+(\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot})^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ}

\displaystyle+(\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot})^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle+(\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot})^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}.

We first note that in what follows, we shall repeatedly expand
\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}
and
\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}
as in Lemma
1
and Lemma
2
. Therefore, if we can show the terms involved in expansions are
o_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}\|_{2}
or
o_{P}(1)(\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij})
, then they are negligible. The reason is that we can repeatedly expand these terms and they will eventually attain a desired order.

We start with
\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}
, and consider

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}-\mathbf{F})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}

(B.29)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\sum_{s=1,\neq 10}^{25}\mathbf{J}_{s}\boldsymbol{\Pi}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}

\displaystyle\coloneqq

\displaystyle-(\mathbf{A}_{ij,1}+\cdots+\mathbf{A}_{ij,24}),

where
\mathbf{J}_{s}
’s are defined in the proof of Lemma
1
,
\boldsymbol{\Pi}=(\mathbf{F}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}/T)^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot\centerdot}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot\centerdot}/(LN))^{-1}
, and the definitions of
\mathbf{A}_{ij,1},\ldots,\mathbf{A}_{ij,24}
should be obvious. First, we show that

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{9}\|\mathbf{A}_{ij,s}\|_{2}=o_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right).

(B.30)

Take
\mathbf{A}_{ij,2}
as an example without loss of generality. Note that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot\centerdot}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}(\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{*}+\mathbf{V}_{ij\centerdot})

(B.31)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}-\mathbf{F})+\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\circ}\mathbf{H}_{i}^{\circ-1}-\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}\mathbf{H}_{j}^{\bullet-1}-\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet})+\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{V}_{ij\centerdot},

where
\mathbf{H}_{i}^{\circ}=\frac{1}{NT}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{i\centerdot}^{\circ\top}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{i\centerdot}^{\circ}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\circ}\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{i}^{\circ-1}
and
\mathbf{H}_{j}^{\bullet}=\frac{1}{LT}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{j\centerdot}^{\bullet\top}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{j\centerdot}^{\bullet}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{j}^{\bullet-1}
. Then we can write

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{ij,2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\frac{1}{LNT}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{L}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{i_{2}j_{2}\centerdot}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{i_{2}j_{2}}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{i_{2}j_{2}})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i_{2}j_{2}}^{\top}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\boldsymbol{\Pi}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\frac{1}{LN}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{L}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{i_{2}j_{2}\centerdot}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i_{2}j_{2}}^{\top}\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot\centerdot}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot\centerdot}}{LN}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{i_{2}j_{2}}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{i_{2}j_{2}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}\|_{2}\cdot(\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{V}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\frac{1}{LN}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{L}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{i_{2}j_{2}\centerdot}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i_{2}j_{2}}^{\top}\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot\centerdot}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot\centerdot}}{LN}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{i_{2}j_{2}}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{i_{2}j_{2}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)

where the first inequality follows from (
B.31
) and Assumption
1
, and the second inequality follows from
\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|=o_{P}(1)
by the third result of Lemma
2
. The rest terms involved in
\sum_{s=1}^{9}\|\mathbf{A}_{ij,s}\|_{2}
can be proved similarly, so we omit the details. Thus, (
B.30
) is proved.

For
\mathbf{A}_{ij,10}
, write

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{A}_{ij,10}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\frac{1}{LNT}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{L}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{N}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i_{2}j_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i_{2}j_{2}}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{F}_{i_{2}}^{\circ\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\boldsymbol{\Pi}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}(\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1})\|_{2}\cdot\frac{1}{LNT}\left\|\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{L}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i_{2}j_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i_{2}j_{2}}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{F}_{i_{2}}^{\circ\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}(\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1})\|_{2},

where the second inequality follows from the development of
\mathbf{J}_{11}
.

For
\mathbf{A}_{ij,11}
, it is easy to show that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{A}_{ij,11}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)

following the development of
\mathbf{A}_{ij,2}
and
\mathbf{J}_{12}
.

Similar to
\mathbf{A}_{ij,10}
and
\mathbf{A}_{ij,11}
, we know that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{A}_{ij,12}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}(\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1})\|_{2},

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{A}_{ij,13}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right),

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{A}_{ij,14}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}(\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1})\|_{2},

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{A}_{ij,15}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right).

Similar to the development of
\mathbf{J}_{17}
to
\mathbf{J}_{25}
, it is easy to know that

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{ij,16}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{L\wedge T}\right),

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{ij,17}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{L\wedge T}\sqrt{N\wedge T}}\right),

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{ij,18}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{L\wedge T}\sqrt{N\wedge T}}\right),

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{ij,19}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{L\wedge T}\right),

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{ij,20}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{L\wedge T}\right),

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{ij,21}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{N\wedge T}\right),

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{ij,22}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{N\wedge T}\right),

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{ij,23}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{N\wedge T}\right),

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{ij,24}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{LN\wedge T}\right).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\|\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}\|_{2}=o_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right).

in view of the condition
\sqrt{T}/(L\wedge N)\to 0
in the body of this theorem.

Next, we consider
\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}
.

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}\mathbf{H}_{j}^{\bullet-1}-\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}

(B.32)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\sum_{s=1,\neq 22}^{25}\mathbf{W}_{j,\ell}\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{j}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle-(\mathbf{B}_{ij,1}+\cdots+\mathbf{B}_{ij,24}),

where
\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{j}=(\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}/T)^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}^{{}^{\bullet}\top}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}^{\bullet}/L)^{-1}
, and the definitions of
\mathbf{B}_{ij,1},\ldots,\mathbf{B}_{ij,24}
should be obvious. In what follows, we consider these terms one by one.

Similar to the development of
\mathbf{A}_{ij,1}
to
\mathbf{A}_{ij,9}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{9}\|\mathbf{B}_{ij,s}\|_{2}=o_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right).

For
\mathbf{B}_{ij,10}
to
\mathbf{B}_{ij,12}
, it is easy to know that

\displaystyle\sum_{s=10}^{12}\|\mathbf{B}_{ij,s}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}\|_{2}

by the development of
\mathbf{W}_{j,10}
to
\mathbf{W}_{j,12}
.

For
\mathbf{B}_{ij,13}
, write

\displaystyle\mathbf{B}_{ij,13}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i_{2}j}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i_{2}j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{j}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}^{\bullet}(\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}^{\bullet\top}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}^{\bullet})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}.

Therefore, the investigation of
\mathbf{B}_{ij,13}
reduces to the study of
\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}
, which infers that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{B}_{ij,13}\|_{2}=o_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right).

Invoking (
B.31
), it is easy to show that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{B}_{ij,14}\|_{2}=o_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right).

For
\mathbf{B}_{ij,15}
and
\mathbf{B}_{ij,16}
, following the development similar to
\mathbf{W}_{j,15}
and
\mathbf{W}_{j,16}
, it is easy to know that

\displaystyle\mathbf{B}_{ij,15}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\|_{2},

\displaystyle\mathbf{B}_{ij,16}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{P}(1)\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\|_{2}.

For
\mathbf{B}_{ij,17}
to
\mathbf{B}_{ij,25}
, invoking (
B.31
) and following the development for
\mathbf{W}_{j,17}
to
\mathbf{W}_{j,25}
, it is easy to know that

\displaystyle\sum_{s=17}^{25}\|\mathbf{B}_{ij,s}\|_{2}=o_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right).

Thus, we can conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\|\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}\|=o_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right).

(B.33)

Further, by noting
i
and
j
are symmetric, we immediately obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\|\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ}\|=o_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right).

Thus, when driving the asymptotic distribution, we only need to focus on the following term

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot},

which in connection with (
B.31
) infers that we just need to focus on

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\mathbf{V}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}.

By standard large block and small block technique (cf.,
Gao, 2007
), we immediately obtain that for
\forall(i,j)
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\mathbf{V}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}\to_{D}N(\mathbf{0},\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},\varepsilon,ij}),

where
\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},\varepsilon,ij}=\lim_{T\to\infty}\frac{1}{T}E[\mathbf{V}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{ij\centerdot}]
. In connection with the fact that for
\forall(i,j)
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}\to_{P}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},ij},

the proof is now completed.

(2) We now investigate the mean group estimators. For each
i
, recall that we have defined
\widetilde{\mathbf{b}}_{i\centerdot}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\widetilde{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}
and
\boldsymbol{\beta}_{i\centerdot}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}
. Additionally, we have

\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}=\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{*}+\mathbf{V}_{ij\centerdot}
,
where
\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}=(\mathbf{F},\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ},\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet})
and
\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{*}=(\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\top},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\circ\top},\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\bullet\top})^{\top}
.

Then, we have the following expansion for
\widetilde{\mathbf{b}}_{i\centerdot}
:

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{b}}_{i\centerdot}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}(\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot})^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{Y}_{ij\centerdot}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\beta}_{i\centerdot}+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}(\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{*\top}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{*}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}(\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot})^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{*}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}(\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{*\top}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}(\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot})^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\beta}_{i\centerdot}+\mathbf{D}_{i,1}+\cdots+\mathbf{D}_{i,4},

where
\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{*}=(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\top},\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ\top},\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top})^{\top}
and the definitions of
\mathbf{D}_{i,1},\ldots,\mathbf{D}_{i,4}
are obvious. In what follows, we study the these terms one by one. For the simplicity of notation, we define
\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}_{ij}=T^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}
.

For
\mathbf{D}_{i,1}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{i,1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},ij}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{*\top}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{*}+\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}_{ij}^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},ij}^{-1}\big)\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{*\top}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{*}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{i,1,1}+\mathbf{D}_{i,1,2}.

To study the order of these terms, we can further write

\displaystyle\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}_{ij}^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\begin{array}[]{c }\big(\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\big)^{\top}\\
\big(\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\circ}\mathbf{H}_{i}^{\circ-1}\big)^{\top}\\
\big(\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}\mathbf{H}_{j}^{\bullet-1}\big)^{\top}\\
\end{array}\right)\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\Big(\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1},\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\circ}\mathbf{H}_{i}^{\circ-1},\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}\mathbf{H}_{j}^{\bullet-1}\Big)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\left(\begin{array}[]{c c c }\mathcal{M}_{ij,11},\cdots,\mathcal{M}_{ij,13}\\
\vdots,\vdots,\vdots\\
\mathcal{M}_{ij,31},\cdots,\mathcal{M}_{ij,33}\\
\end{array}\right),

where the first equality holds by the orthogonality between
\widehat{\mathbf{C}}
,
\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\circ}
and
\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}
,
\mathbf{H}=\ \frac{1}{LNT}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot\centerdot}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot\centerdot}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\widehat{\mathbf{V}}^{-1}
,
\mathbf{H}_{i}^{\circ}=\frac{1}{NT}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{i\centerdot}^{\circ\top}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{i\centerdot}^{\circ}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{i}^{\circ}\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{i}^{\circ-1}
and
\mathbf{H}_{j}^{\bullet}=\frac{1}{LT}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}^{\bullet\top}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}^{\bullet}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{j}^{\bullet-1}
. Here,
\mathcal{M}_{ij,11}=(\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1})^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}(\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1})
, and the other block matrices are defined in a similar manner.

Then, for the term with
\mathcal{M}_{ij,11}
, the expansion in (
B.19
) gives

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},ij}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\top}\mathcal{M}_{ij,11}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},ij}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\top}\Big(\sum_{s_{1}\neq 10}^{25}\mathbf{J}_{s_{1}}\boldsymbol{\Pi}\Big)^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\Big(\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathbf{J}_{s_{2}}\boldsymbol{\Pi}\Big)\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}

(B.36)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{1}\neq 10}^{25}\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,s_{1}s_{2}},

where
\boldsymbol{\Pi}=(\mathbf{F}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}/T)^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot\centerdot}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot\centerdot}/(LN))^{-1}
and
\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,s_{1}s_{2}}=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},ij}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\top}\mathbf{J}_{s_{1}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{J}_{s_{2}}\boldsymbol{\Pi}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}
.

For the term with
\mathbf{J}_{1}
, simple algebra yields that

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,1s_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\max_{j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\|_{2}\right),

which is negligible.
For the term with
\mathbf{J}_{2}
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{J}_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{LNT}\sum_{i,j}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\top}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}+\frac{1}{LNT}\sum_{i,j}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\circ}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\top}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{LNT}\sum_{i,j}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\bullet}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\top}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}+\frac{1}{LNT}\sum_{i,j}\mathbf{V}_{ij\centerdot}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\top}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\mathbf{J}_{2,1}+\cdots+\mathbf{J}_{2,4}.

For
\mathbf{J}_{2,1}
, it is clear to see that

\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{j=1}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},ij}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\top}\mathbf{J}_{2,1}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\Big(\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathbf{J}_{s_{2}}\boldsymbol{\Pi}\Big)\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}=O_{P}\left(\max_{j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\cdot\frac{1}{T}\|\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\|^{2}_{2}\right).

(B.37)

For the second term in
\mathbf{J}_{2}
, let
\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{\beta,j}=(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{1j}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{1j}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{1j})^{\top}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{1j}^{\circ\top},\cdots,\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{Lj}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{Lj}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{Lj})^{\top}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{Lj}^{\circ\top})^{\top}
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{LNT}\left\|\sum_{i,j}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\circ}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\top}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{LNT}\left\|\sum_{j}\mathbf{F}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{\beta,j}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\right\|_{2}

(B.38)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{L}}\left\|\mathbf{F}^{\circ}\right\|_{2}\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{\sqrt{L\vee T}}{\sqrt{L}}\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\right),

where the last equality holds by Assumption
1
. It further yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{j=1}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},ij}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\top}\mathbf{J}_{2,2}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\Big(\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathbf{J}_{s_{2}}\boldsymbol{\Pi}\Big)\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{L\wedge T}}\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\|_{2}\right).

Analogously, the following result holds for
\mathbf{J}_{2,3}
:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{LNT}\left\|\sum_{i,j}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\bullet}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\top}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{\sqrt{N\vee T}}{\sqrt{N}}\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\right),

which immediately gives

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{j=1}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},ij}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\top}\mathbf{J}_{2,3}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\Big(\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathbf{J}_{s_{2}}\boldsymbol{\Pi}\Big)\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N\wedge T}}\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\|_{2}\right).

For
\mathbf{J}_{2,4}
, by Assumption
1
,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{J}_{2,4}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{LNT}\left\|\mathbf{V}_{\centerdot\centerdot\centerdot}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Phi}^{\dagger}_{\beta}\mathbf{F}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\right\|_{2}

(B.39)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{LN}}\left\|\mathbf{V}_{\centerdot\centerdot\centerdot}\right\|_{2}\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{\sqrt{LN\vee T}}{\sqrt{LN}}\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\right),

where
\boldsymbol{\Phi}^{\dagger}_{\beta}=(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{11}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{11}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{11})^{\top},\cdots,\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{LN}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{LN}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{LN})^{\top})^{\top}
. In summary, these results can jointly imply that
\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,2s_{2}}
is negligible.

For
\mathbf{J}_{3}=\frac{1}{LNT}\sum_{i,j}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}
, considering
\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}=\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}(\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1})
, we have

\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,3s_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\cdot\frac{1}{T}\|\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\|^{2}_{2}\right).

(B.40)

For
\mathbf{J}_{4}
, we write

\displaystyle\mathbf{J}_{4}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{LNT}\sum_{i,j}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}+\frac{1}{LNT}\sum_{i,j}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\circ}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{LNT}\sum_{i,j}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\bullet}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}+\frac{1}{LNT}\sum_{i,j}\mathbf{V}_{ij\centerdot}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\mathbf{J}_{4,1}+\cdots+\mathbf{J}_{4,4}.

For
\mathbf{J}_{4,1}
, using analogous arguments to those in (
B.37
) and (
B.40
), we can readily obtain

\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{j=1}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},ij}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\top}\mathbf{J}_{4,1}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\Big(\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathbf{J}_{s_{2}}\boldsymbol{\Pi}\Big)\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}=O_{P}\left(\max_{j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\cdot\frac{1}{T}\|\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\|^{2}_{2}\right).

(B.41)

Define
\boldsymbol{\Phi}^{\ast}_{\beta,j}=(T^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{1}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}^{\circ}_{1j}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{1j}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{1j})^{\top}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{1j}^{\circ\top},\cdots,T^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{L}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}^{\circ}_{Lj}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{Lj}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{Lj})^{\top}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{Lj}^{\circ\top})^{\top}
. By Assumption
1
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{LNT}\left\|\sum_{i,j}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\circ}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{LN}\left\|\sum_{j}\mathbf{F}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{\beta,j}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{L}}\left\|\mathbf{F}^{\circ}\right\|_{2}\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{\sqrt{L\vee T}}{\sqrt{L}}\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\right).

Therefore, it holds for
\mathbf{J}_{4,2}
that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},ij}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\top}\mathbf{J}_{4,2}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\Big(\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathbf{J}_{s_{2}}\boldsymbol{\Pi}\Big)\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{L\wedge T}}\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\|_{2}\right).

Analogously, for
\mathbf{J}_{4,3}
and
\mathbf{J}_{4,4}
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},ij}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\top}\mathbf{J}_{4,3}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\Big(\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathbf{J}_{s_{2}}\boldsymbol{\Pi}\Big)\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N\wedge T}}\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\|_{2}\right),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},ij}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\top}\mathbf{J}_{4,L}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\Big(\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathbf{J}_{s_{2}}\boldsymbol{\Pi}\Big)\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{L\wedge T}}\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\|_{2}\right).

In summary of (
B.41
), (
B.4
), and (
B.4
),

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,4s_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{L\wedge N\wedge T}}\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\|_{2}\right),

Analogously, we can show that
\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,5s_{2}}
,
\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,6s_{2}}
and
\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,7s_{2}}
are all negligible. For
\mathbf{J}_{8}
and
\mathbf{J}_{9}
, together with the condition
\|\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{\centerdot\centerdot\centerdot}\|_{2}=O_{P}(LN\wedge T)
, similar arguments to those in (
B.39
) can be applied here to show that

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,8s_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{LN\wedge T}}\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\|_{2}\right),

and

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,9s_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{LN\wedge T}}\max_{i,j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\|_{2}\right).

Let
\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{\gamma,j}=(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{1j}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{1j}^{\circ\top},\cdots,\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{Lj}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{Lj}^{\circ\top})^{\top}
. For
\mathbf{J}_{11}
, it follows Assumption
1
that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{LN}\left\|\sum_{i,j}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ\top}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{LN}\sum_{j}\left\|\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{\gamma,j}\right\|\cdot\left\|\mathbf{F}^{\circ}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{\sqrt{L\vee T}}{\sqrt{L}}\right).

Together with the fact
\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{F}=\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}(\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1})
, it yields

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,11s_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{L\wedge T}}\cdot\frac{1}{T}\|\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\|^{2}_{2}\right).

Using analogous arguments, we can obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,13s_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N\wedge T}}\cdot\frac{1}{T}\|\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\|^{2}_{2}\right).

For
\mathbf{J}_{15}
and
\mathbf{J}_{16}
, since
\left\|\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{\centerdot\centerdot\centerdot}\right\|_{2}=O_{P}(\sqrt{LN\vee T})
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{LN}\left\|\sum_{i,j}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{ij\centerdot}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\top}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{LN}\left\|\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}_{\centerdot\centerdot\centerdot}\right\|_{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot\centerdot}\right\|_{2}

(B.44)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{\sqrt{LN\vee T}}{\sqrt{LN}}\right),

which immediately yields

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,15s_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{LN\wedge T}}\cdot\frac{1}{T}\|\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\|^{2}_{2}\right),

and

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,16s_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{LN\wedge T}}\cdot\frac{1}{T}\|\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\|^{2}_{2}\right).

Analogously, we can show that
\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,20s_{2}}
,
\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,21s_{2}}
,
\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,23s_{2}}
,
\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,24s_{2}}
and
\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,25s_{2}}
are all negligible. Let
\boldsymbol{\Phi}^{\ast}_{\gamma,j}=\big(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{1j}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{1j}^{\bullet\top},\cdots,\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{Lj}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{Lj}^{\bullet\top}\big)^{\top}
and
\boldsymbol{\Phi}^{\ast}_{\gamma}=(\boldsymbol{\Phi}^{\ast}_{\gamma,1},\cdots,\boldsymbol{\Phi}^{\ast}_{\gamma,N})
. For
\mathbf{J}_{17}
, it holds by Assumption
1
that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{LNT}\left\|\sum_{i,j}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ\top}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ\top}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{LNT}\left\|\mathbf{F}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\Phi}^{\ast}_{\gamma}\mathbf{F}^{\circ\top}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{LNT}\left\|\mathbf{F}^{\bullet}\right\|_{2}\left\|\mathbf{F}^{\circ}\right\|_{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{\Phi}^{\ast}_{\gamma}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{L\wedge T}\sqrt{N\wedge T}}\right).

Therefore,

\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,17s_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{L\wedge T}\sqrt{N\wedge T}}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\|_{2}\right).

(B.45)

Analogously to (
B.45
),
\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,18s_{2}}
,
\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,19s_{2}}
and
\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,22s_{2}}
are negligible.

For the terms with
\mathbf{J}_{12}
and
\mathbf{J}_{14}
, by (
B.20
) and (
B.21
),

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,12s_{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{L\wedge T}}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\|_{2}\right),

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{2}\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,14s_{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N\wedge T}}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\|\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\|_{2}\right).

Following from the established results, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},ij}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\top}\mathcal{M}_{ij,11}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{1}=12,14}\sum_{s_{2}=12,14}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,11,s_{1}s_{2}}+o_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),

(B.46)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},ij}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\top}\mathcal{R}_{F}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathcal{R}_{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}+o_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),

where
\mathcal{R}_{F}=\sum_{ij}(\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\circ}+\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot\centerdot}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot\centerdot})^{-1}
, and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},ij}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\top}\mathcal{R}_{F}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathcal{R}_{F}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}\right\|=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{L\wedge N\wedge T}\right).

(B.47)

We now proceed to investigate the remaining terms in
\mathbf{D}_{i,1,1}
.
Noteworthily,
\mathcal{M}_{ij,22}
,
\mathcal{M}_{ij,33}
,
\mathcal{M}_{ij,12}
,
\mathcal{M}_{ij,21}
,
\mathcal{M}_{ij,13}
,
\mathcal{M}_{ij,31}
,
\mathcal{M}_{ij,23}
and
\mathcal{M}_{ij,32}
have similar structures. To avoid repetitive and tedious calculations, we only provide detailed derivations for
\mathcal{M}_{ij,13}
. For the term with
\mathcal{M}_{ij,13}
,
By (
B.19
) and (
B.23
),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},ij}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathcal{M}_{ij,31}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},ij}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\Big(\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}\mathbf{H}_{j}^{\bullet-1}\Big)^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}(\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},ij}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\Big(\sum_{s_{1}=1,\neq 22}^{25}\mathbf{W}_{j,s_{1}}\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{j}\Big)^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\Big(\sum_{s_{2}=1,\neq 10}^{25}\mathbf{J}_{s_{2}}\boldsymbol{\Pi}\Big)\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{1}=1,\neq 22}^{25}\sum_{s_{2}=1,\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,31,s_{1}s_{2}},

where
\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,31,s_{1}s_{2}}=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},ij}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{j,s_{1}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{ij}}\mathbf{J}_{s_{2}}\boldsymbol{\Pi}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}
,
\mathbf{H}_{j}^{\bullet}=(LT)^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{j\centerdot}^{\bullet\top}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{j\centerdot}^{\bullet}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{j}^{\bullet-1}
and
\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{j}=(\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}/T)^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}^{{}^{\bullet}\top}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{\centerdot j}^{\bullet}/L)^{-1}
.

Additionally, directly applying the results for
\mathbf{W}_{j,1},\cdots,\mathbf{W}_{j,5}
in the equations from (
B.24
) to (
B.25
), we obtain that
\sum_{s_{2}=1,\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,31,1s_{2}},\cdots,\sum_{s_{2}=1,\neq 10}^{25}\mathcal{M}^{\ast}_{i,31,5s_{2}}
are all negligible. For the term with
\mathbf{W}_{j,6}=\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{X}_{ij\centerdot}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}
, we write

\displaystyle\mathbf{W}_{j,6}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\circ}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\bullet}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{V}_{ij\centerdot}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{W}_{j,6,1}+\cdots+\mathbf{W}_{j,6,4}.

For
\mathbf{W}_{j,6,1}
, it is clear to see that

\displaystyle\mathbf{W}_{j,6,1}=\frac{1}{LT}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}(\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1})\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{F}_{j}^{\bullet\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{j}^{\bullet},

and therefore

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\left\|\sum_{i_{2},j}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{v},i_{1}j}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{i_{1}j}^{\bullet\top}\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{j,6,1}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{i_{1}j}}(\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i_{1}j}\right\|=O_{P}\left(\max_{j}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij}\|\cdot\frac{1}{T}\|\mathbf{F}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\|^{2}_{2}\right).

Similarly to (
B.38
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{LN}\left\|\sum_{i}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}}\mathbf{F}_{i}^{\circ}\boldsymbol{\phi}_{ij}^{\circ}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{ij}-\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{ij})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{ij}^{\bullet\top}\right\|_{2}
